  main.py:8067:# Purpose:
  main.py:8068:#   1. Stop any single position from

  main.py:9043:# QFOS PORTFOLIO ACCOUNTING RECONCILER
  main.py:9044:# Purpose:
  main.py:9045:#   DB-level watchdog/profit-engine exits 
correctly close paper
> main.py:9046:#   positions, but they may bypass the

> main.py:9047:#   This reconciler fixes

  main.py:9048:#
  main.py:9049:# Formula:
  main.py:9050:#   realized_pnl   = SUM(trades.pnl)
> main.py:9051:#   unrealized_pnl = SUM(open 
positions.unrealized_pnl)
> main.py:9052:#   exposure       = SUM(open 
positions.exposure or qty * last_price)
> main.py:9053:#   equity         = starting_equity +

  main.py:9054:#   cash           = equity - exposure
